- **turbo-plugin-git-svn**: PreToolUse, SessionStart on *, Bash. turbo-plugin-git-svn: SessionStart 提示性 prompt（marker 缺失時提示 /tp-setup）+ git commit 前提示套用 tp-commit-msg。兩者皆 advisory（不會 block session、不會擋下指令、不會代為授予權限）。 ([Bryant-Tang/turbo-plugins-claude](https://github.com/Bryant-Tang/turbo-plugins-claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/turbo-plugin-git-svn/hooks/hooks.json))
- **turbo-plugin-multi-repo-workspace**: WorktreeCreate, WorktreeRemove. turbo-plugin-multi-repo-workspace: WorktreeCreate / WorktreeRemove。讓隔離工作副本在「本身不是 repo、底下並排多個 repo」的工作區根也能建立——交還一份工作區鏡像，其子目錄是各專案真正的 worktree，所以 session 仍站在根目錄、`git -C &lt;專案&gt;` 照樣打得到每一個專案，而沒有任何東西動到主 checkout。注意：設了這組 hook 之後，一般 repo 的隔離工作副本也走這裡（實測確認），腳本內含對應的分支。 ([Bryant-Tang/turbo-plugins-claude](https://github.com/Bryant-Tang/turbo-plugins-claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/turbo-plugin-multi-repo-workspace/hooks/hooks.json))
- **turbo-plugin-three-environment-db**: SessionStart on *. turbo-plugin-three-environment-db: SessionStart advisory hook — 當 peer worktree 缺少 .turbo-plugin/dbhub.local.toml(tp-dbhub MCP server 將無法啟動)時提示。僅 advisory(不會 block session);專案未使用 db 時 no-op。 ([Bryant-Tang/turbo-plugins-claude](https://github.com/Bryant-Tang/turbo-plugins-claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/turbo-plugin-three-environment-db/hooks/hooks.json))
